| `pc-bd7a8a362df4` | length: ums_admin_login_log.address has length 100 | `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY` | no | not-observed | not assessed | not-assessed | 0.80 | — | Predicate 'ums_admin_login_log.address has length 100'. The only write path to ums_admin_login_log is POST /user/login (business-oauth2) -> MessageService.sendAdminLoginLog -> RocketMQ topic admin-login-log-topic -> message-admin-login-log consumer -> UmsAdminLoginLogService.insert. It needs a RocketMQ broker, a Redis-backed OAuth2 token endpoint on a hard-coded localhost:9001, and an external IP-geolocation call (http://ip.taobao.com) that populates address. ## Deployment

- Base URLs: `http://127.0.0.1:18205`

| Purpose | Working directory | Command | Exit |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: |
| build | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/application` | `JAVA_HOME=/usr/lib/jvm/java-8-openjdk-amd64 mvn -q -B -DskipTests -pl business/business-reg-service,provider/provider-admin-service -am install` | 0 |
| fetch junixsocket for Unix-socket JDBC | `/tmp/mysp-verify` | `mvn -q -B -f /tmp/mysp-verify/pom.xml dependency:copy-dependencies -DexcludeGroupIds=mysql -DoutputDirectory=/tmp/mysp-verify/extlib` | 0 |
| repackage deploy jars without sentinel-transport-simple-http | `/tmp/mysp-verify/deploy` | `zip -d <jar> BOOT-INF/lib/sentinel-transport-simple-http-1.6.3.jar` | 0 |
| init database | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/application` | `mysqld --no-defaults --initialize-insecure --datadir=/tmp/mysp-verify/data` | 0 |
| start database | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/application` | `mysqld --no-defaults --datadir=/tmp/mysp-verify/data --socket=/tmp/mysp-verify/run/mysql.sock --skip-networking --mysqlx=OFF --general-log=1 ...` | — |
| load schema | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/application` | `mysql -S /tmp/mysp-verify/run/mysql.sock -uroot -e 'create database mplus_ums_admin default charset utf8' && mysql -S ... mplus_ums_admin < docs/sql/mplus.sql` | 0 |
| create loopback-only network namespace | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/application` | `unshare -rn sh -c 'ip link set lo up; exec sleep 100000'` | — |
| start provider-admin-service (Dubbo 20882) | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/output/logs` | `nsenter -t <holder> -U -n --preserve-credentials /tmp/mysp-verify/start-provider.sh (see logs/start-provider.sh)` | — |
| start business-reg-service (HTTP 18205) | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/output` | `nsenter -t <holder> -U -n --preserve-credentials /tmp/mysp-verify/start-reg.sh (see logs/start-reg.sh)` | — |
| run verification harness | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/output` | `nsenter -t <holder> -U -n --preserve-credentials python3 /tmp/mysp-verify/harness.py <output>` | 0 |
| stop | `/artifact/results/RQ1/funtl_MyShopPlus/funtl_MyShopPlus-20260929T160737Z-ae489bc7/output` | `kill <provider/reg java pids>; kill <namespace holder>; mysqladmin -S /tmp/mysp-verify/run/mysql.sock -uroot shutdown` | 0 |

## Warnings

- The first harness run (evidence in raw/attempt1, logs/attempt1) failed before persistence because the direct Dubbo URL lacked version=1.0.0. It was fixed and all cases were rerun. Only the rerun is used as evidence.
- The runtime database is MySQL 8.0.42 (strict mode) and not the TiDB the project was designed for.
- 1116 of 1134 targets are on tables that no application code writes (or only through blocked external infrastructure).
